head -c 20000 /home/zhengyew/telelean/verbatim_prompt_log/bundle_2/user_requested_documents/05_downloads_and_installs.md
  => err=False # Everything downloaded or installed during this session (turns 1 and 2)
     
     Requested in mid-turn message 1 of turn 2: *"give me an exhaustive list of things that you / your subagents downloaded or installed; check thoroughly that the list is exhaustive."*
     
     **Method.** The audit was read-only and nothing was deleted. It took four steps:
     1. Two independent inventories:
        - **from the transcripts:** every command run by me or any of the 26 subagents that had run before the audit, wherever it could reach the network, plus every web fetch. The audit's own 3 agents were read-only;
        - **from the disk:** files whose inode change time falls after the session started (2026-11-01 01:00 +0800), in `$HOME`, `/tmp` and `/var`. Inode change time also catches archive extractions, which keep their original modification times.
     2. A third agent reconciled the two inventories: every download needs a disk trace, and every disk item needs a source command.
     3. That agent checked each mismatch itself.
     4. The reports are in `../subagent_reports/audit_workflow/`.
     
     The reconciliation caught things a single method missed, including:
     - the `/var/crash` report;
     - the test VS Code's dictionary downloads;
     - the Copilot logs;
     - Chrome's own model downloads during a turn-1 benchmark;
     - research downloads made by Python scripts rather than curl or npm.
     
     ## Summary
     
     | # | What | Where | Size | Turn | Still there? | Needed by telelean? |
     |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
     | 1 | VS Code 1.140.0 test build (+ test pr

  | import itertools
  | def solve(A,b):
  |     n=len(A[0])
  |     AtA=[[sum(A[k][i]*A[k][j] for k in range(len(A))) for j in range(n)] for i in range(n)]
  |     Atb=[sum(A[k][i]*b[k] for k in range(len(A))) for i in range(n)]
  |     # gaussian elimination
  |     M=[AtA[i]+[Atb[i]] for i in range(n)]
  |     for c in range(n):
  |         p=max(range(c,n), key=lambda r: abs(M[r][c])); M[c],M[p]=M[p],M[c]
  |         for r in range(n):
  |             if r!=c:
  |                 f=M[r][c]/M[c][c]
  |                 M[r]=[M[r][j]-f*M[c][j] for j in range(n+1)]
  |     return [M[i][n]/M[i][i] for i in range(n)]
  | # scale columns to per-MTok
  | A=[[r[0]/1e6, r[1]/1e6, r[2]/1e6, r[3]/1e6, r[4]/1e6] for r in rows]
  | b=[r[5] for r in rows]
  | # input tokens are tiny -> fix input price from ratio; solve out, cr, c5, c1 only
  | A2=[[a[1],a[2],a[3],a[4]] for a in A]
  | x=solve(A2,b)
  | print('per-MTok estimates: out=%.3f cr=%.4f c5=%.4f c1=%.4f'%tuple(x))

     export async function checkUserNamespaces(host: Partial<HostTools> = {}): Promise<string | undefined> {
       const tools: HostTools = { ...defaultHostTools(), ...host };
       if (usernsOk.has(tools.bwrap)) return undefined;
       const args = [
         '--ro-bind', '/usr', '/usr',
         '--symlink', 'usr/lib', '/lib',
         '--symlink', 'usr/lib64', '/lib64',
         '--symlink', 'usr/bin', '/bin',
         '--proc', '/proc',
         '--dev', '/dev',
         '--unshare-all',
         '--die-with-parent',
         '--new-session',
         '--clearenv',
         '--', '/usr/bin/true',
       ];
       const r = await execCapture(tools.bwrap, args, { PATH: '/usr/bin:/bin' }, 10_000);
       if (r.code === 0) {
         usernsOk.add(tools.bwrap);
         return undefined;
       }
       const why = lastLines(r.stderr, 3) || r.error || `exit ${String(r.code)}`;
       return `bwrap cannot set up the sandbox here (${why})${usernsHint(r.stderr)}`;
     }
